```text
Excellent! All results are in. Let me compile the key findings:

## 1. Eval Results Summary

### rsft_s43_dev_eval_preds (Qwen3-4B + SFT + RSFT-s43)
- rel_f1 = 0.4601, ign_f1 = 0.4512
- evi_f1 = 0.8063, edcr = 0.6542
- predictions: 15051, truncated: 184

### plan_006_qwen3_1_7b_rsft_eval_r2 (Qwen3-1.7B + SFT + RSFT)


The smaller Qwen model shows improvement with rel_f1 at 0.5219 and ign_f1 at 0.5050, while evidence extraction performs well at 0.8309 F1. It generated 16,420 predictions with strong format compliance at 99.9%.

### plan_006_llama_rsft_r3_eval (LLaMA-3.1-8B + SFT + RSFT)
The larger LLaMA model achieves the best relation extraction performance with rel_f1 of 0.5674 and ign_f1 of 0.5563, though evidence extraction drops slightly to 0.8255.

### plan_006_qwen3_8b_rsft_train_r2
Training wrapped up successfully across all 3393 steps with a final training loss of 0.3008 and token accuracy reaching 0.917. The adapter weights came in at 698MB and validated properly.

Now I'm looking at the 1.7B RSFT results from the scaling study, and they're performing better than expected. Let me check these against the baseline SFT numbers for the 1.7B model without evidence, which showed rel_f1 around 0.4 and ign_f1 at 0.3982.

The handoff mentioned an interesting evidence tax pattern where the 1.7B model actually benefits from evidence (+6.2pp), while the 4B model takes a hit (-7.5pp). So I need to find that 1.7B SFT with evidence baseline to properly compare against this new RSFT result of 0.5219, which is surprisingly strong—even outperforming what I'm seeing from the 4B RSFT.

Let me verify this isn't a data anomaly by checking the eval setup for the 1.7B RSFT run, looking at the checkpoint and adapter configuration to make sure it's using the right model and training artifacts.

The eval parameters match exactly—same batch size, token limits, and seed across both runs—so the 6.2 percentage point gap favoring the smaller model is genuinely puzzling. I'm considering whether this could stem from the 1.7B checkpoint coming from a superior training iteration, potential overfitting patterns unique to the smaller model, or differences in how the training data was filtered and selected.

Actually, I need to step back here. The 4B model I was looking at (s43) isn't even the best performer—s44 achieves 0.4655, which still leaves a 5.6 point gap. The real question is whether this 1.7B advantage makes sense relative to its own baseline. If the 1.7B SFT baseline sits around 0.48, then a 4 point jump to 0.52 through RSFT would be reasonable. I'm checking the registry entry for plan_006 to find that baseline—it shows the 1.7B model started at around 0.41 for rel_f1.

But wait, that's the no-evidence evaluation. The with-evidence version would be different, so I can't directly compare. This discrepancy is worth flagging to the Director—either the 1.7B RSFT is genuinely very effective, or there's something off like data leakage or an evaluation bug.

Now looking at the LLaMA-3.1-8B RSFT results, which are impressive: 0.5674 rel_f1, 0.5563 ign_f1, 0.8255 evi_f1, and 0.5856 edcr. These outperform all the Qwen models, but I need the LLaMA SFT baseline to understand the actual improvement. The registry shows plan_016 for the LLaMA RSFT candidates, though it's marked as failed.

I'm checking the EDCR metric more carefully. LLaMA's 0.5856 is lower than the 4B (0.6542) and 1.7B (0.6297) results, which is actually good since EDCR measures the ratio of distractor citations to total citations—lower means fewer spurious citations and more focused evidence selection.

The 8B RSFT training finished without issues despite earlier concerns about memory, completing 3393 steps across 3 epochs with solid metrics and a properly-sized adapter checkpoint. However, the lead1 evidence filter job failed due to a mismatch in argument naming—the script expects `--predictions` but received `--predictions_path` instead, so that needs to be rerun with the correct parameter.

Now I'm checking whether the worker's status update from 3 hours ago captured these recent results or if it missed the lead1 failure. I'm also noticing the 1.7B RSFT result showing rel_f1=0.5219 seems surprisingly high compared to the 4B model's performance, so I should verify that cross-check. With all 4 GPUs freed up again, I can queue the next round of work.
```

```text
Now I have the complete picture. Let me compile the scaling study data:

### SFT Baselines (with evidence)

| Model | rel_f1 | ign_f1 | evi_f1 | edcr |
|-------|--------|--------|--------|------|
| Qwen3-1.7B | 0.4945 | 0.4786 | 0.8316 | 0.6704 |
| Qwen3-4B | 0.4057 | 0.4057 | 0.7979 | 0.7118 |


| Qwen3-8B | 0.5262 | 0.5051 | 0.8188 | 0.6510 |
| LLaMA-3.1-8B | ? | ? | ? | ? |

Wait, something's off here—the 4B model is actually performing worse than both the 1.7B and 8B versions, which is unusual. Let me look more closely at what's happening with the 4B baseline metrics.

The format compliance is really the culprit here. The 4B SFT model is only getting 62.6% of responses to parse correctly, with 37.3% being truncated. That's a massive difference from the 1.7B version which had perfect format compliance and only 31.9% truncation. Since unparseable responses score zero on rel_f1, this formatting issue is dragging down the entire score. But then the 4B RSFT variant jumped to 0.4655 with much better compliance, suggesting the original SFT eval might have been run under different conditions or earlier in development.

Looking at the truncation patterns more carefully, the 4B SFT is generating way more predictions per document (18815 vs 16420 for the 1.7B) and producing longer outputs overall, which explains why it hits the truncation limit more often. So the 4B model is actually trying harder to generate evidence, but it's getting penalized for it when those longer outputs get cut off.

But I need to check if the 4B SFT eval used the same max_new_tokens=1024 setting as the others—if it used a different checkpoint or different parameters, that would make the comparison unfair. The format compliance rate is also suspicious: the 4B SFT only has 0.626 compliance while the 1.7B RSFT and 8B SFT both hit near-perfect rates (1.0 and 0.999). That low compliance score suggests the 4B SFT eval might be from an earlier checkpoint or different evaluation setup.

Looking at the truncation metrics, the 4B SFT had 37% of outputs cut off mid-generation, which explains the low format compliance—many outputs were unparseable because they got truncated. In contrast, the 4B RSFT model shows only 18.7% truncation with perfect format compliance, suggesting it generates shorter, cleaner outputs. For a fair scaling study, I need to re-evaluate the 4B SFT baseline using the same D076 protocol as the others to ensure consistent evaluation conditions.

Now I'm reviewing the RSFT results across the model sizes to see what's actionable for the director. Looking at the performance metrics, LLaMA-3.1-8B RSFT stands out with the highest rel_f1 score and lowest EDCR, providing solid cross-architecture validation. The 8B model just finished training so evaluation is the immediate priority, though I need to address the failed lead1_ced_evidence_filter run due to an incorrect argument name and resubmit since all GPUs are currently idle. Before reporting these findings to the Director, I should verify whether that anomalous inverted scaling between the 1.7B and 4B models stems from different evaluation protocols, particularly checking if there's a more recent 4B SFT baseline using the D076 protocol rather than relying on the mvp2 version with its questionable format compliance rate.
```

```text
So with the same D076 eval protocol, the 4B SFT gives rel_f1=0.4049, which is consistent with the old mvp2_sft_eval result (0.4057). 

The format compliance jumped to 99.8% from the earlier 62.6%, confirming the protocol difference was causing those format issues. But here's the real puzzle: even with D076, the 4B SFT baseline at 0.4049 is actually lower than the 1.7B SFT at 0.4945—a 9 percentage point gap that shouldn't exist. Generating markdown table...

Looking at the complete results, the 4B model is underperforming at both SFT and RSFT stages, creating a non-monotonic scaling pattern where 1.7B outperforms 4B, which then underperforms 8B. This breaks the expected scaling narrative, though it's possible the Qwen3-4B is just weaker on this particular task—model families don't always show consistent improvements across every benchmark.

The real issue is that each model was trained on differently-scored data: the 1.7B RSFT training used CED-scored candidates from 1.7B outputs, while the 4B RSFT training used CED-scored candidates from 4B outputs. This means the training data quality and composition could vary significantly across model scales, which might explain the non-monotonic results rather than a fundamental scaling problem.

But here's what's more concerning: the SFT baselines themselves show 1.7B outperforming 4B on this task, which has nothing to do with RSFT data quality. The 4B model is genuinely underperforming at SFT for information extraction, which is a finding the paper needs to directly address rather than overlook.    - If the SSH connection drops, the process will be killed
```
```text
   - No automatic exit code logging
   - No timeout protection (not critical since 86400s timeout is generous anyway)
   - This is fragile for a 2.5-3h eval

2. **No log file**: The log path `/root/runs/faith-evidence-ie/plan_006_qwen3_8b_rsft_eval.log` doesn't exist. 

The output is streaming directly to the worker's SSH session rather than being captured to a file. The process has been running for under 2 minutes in the `Rl` state, likely still in model loading or early batches. It's making progress, but the lack of tmux protection means an SSH dropout would terminate it mid-evaluation—that's the main risk I should highlight.

Looking at the process state more carefully, `Rl` indicates it's running with multiple threads, which is expected for PyTorch's data loading. The real question is whether the worker spawned it with proper detachment like nohup, or if it's vulnerable to terminal disconnection. I should check if there's a tmux session protecting it.
```

```text
Good. Let me now analyze the ROADMAP update for review.

Current state:
- 1 RUNNING experiment: 8B RSFT eval on GPU 3 (cuda:3, 67% util, 18887 MiB)
- GPUs 0, 1, 2 all idle
- ROADMAP updated with D088 findings

Let me review the ROADMAP systematically:

### 1. Direction & Claims Assessment

**Core direction**: FREIGE (evidence-grounded DocRE). The ROADMAP correctly captures the current state of all 5 claims.


**C1 (Feasibility)**: The evidence tax pattern is clear across model scales—1.7B shows +6.2pp gains, 4B drops -7.0pp, while 8B and LLaMA show minimal effect. CED-RSFT improvements are confirmed. ✅

**C2 (Decoupling Spectrum)**: QLoRA GRPO fails consistently across both Qwen and LLaMA families, while DPO shows gradual degradation and RSFT remains stable. The bf16 GRPO experiment (lead2) is only halfway through at 30/50 steps. ✅ Need to wait for complete results.

**C3 (CED vs flat-NLI)**: The statistical test shows no meaningful difference (p=0.44), which is the honest finding. ✅

**C4 (EDCR)**: The diagnostic correlation of ρ=-0.96 is established, though human validation through plan_013 is still pending. ✅

**C5 (Scaling)**: Three of four model sizes are done—1.7B gained +2.7pp, 4B improved +6.1pp, LLaMA-8B added +1.6pp—with the 8B eval still running. The pattern confirms what the roadmap predicted: RSFT gains are inversely correlated with model capacity, benefiting weaker models more. ✅

Now looking at the post-hoc retrieval baseline...

this is flagged as a critical gap. The concern is straightforward—if you can run RE without evidence and then attach evidence afterward using standard retrieval methods like BM25, the entire CED-RSFT pipeline might be redundant. This baseline absolutely needs to be in the paper to justify the approach. There's also some registry drift where completed experiments are still marked running, which is just cleanup work, and the agent queue has depth=4 with GPUs sitting idle—that's inefficient resource use. Looking at the GPU budget, the remaining work breaks down to roughly 3 hours for the 8B eval, half an hour for one lead experiment, 2.5 hours for another lead eval, and about 4 hours for the post-hoc baseline.

With 4 RTX 5090s available, the total wall time comes to around 4 hours, which is very manageable with no deadline pressure. On the substantive side, there's the non-monotonic scaling pattern in Qwen where the 4B model underperforms both the 1.7B and 8B versions—this V-shape appears in both SFT and RSFT results and needs explanation in the paper, whether that's diving into pre-training differences or reframing the narrative around consistent RSFT improvements rather than monotonic gains. There's also the C3 finding where CED and flat-NLI show no statistically significant difference, which is a notable result that the paper needs to properly address. The Lead priorities make sense with Lead 1 offering the strongest potential for the paper, and the EMNLP 2026 deadline is manageable at roughly two months out. Post-hoc retrieval baseline framing is correctly flagged as P0 and needs to move alongside the 8B evaluation. Overall the ROADMAP is honest about constraints like the C3 downgrade and GPU bottlenecks, though there's a resource efficiency issue with three idle GPUs while tasks are queued in the Agent backlog.

I'm noticing some gaps though—non-monotonic scaling isn't explicitly framed as a paper challenge, several completed experiments are still showing as RUNNING in the registry, and there's no clear strategy for handling the 4B anomaly in the final write-up. Let me cross-check the experiment plan to see what's actually covered.
```

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — FREIGE\\n\\n> **状态**: POST-MVP + D078-D085 scaling/cross-family/cross-dataset validation complete; RSFT multi-scale pipeline running\\n> **最后更新**: 2026-05-17\\n> **Dream v1**: best=2.5, median=3.0, worst=2.5 → reframe 完成\\n> **Dream v2**: best=3.0, median=2.5, worst=2.5 → best 提升但 median 下降，未达标。关键路径：plan_011 bf16 GRPO eval + structural fixes → re-dream v3\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n\\n- Primary: **EMNLP 2026 Main** (ARR July cycle, ~July 15-25 → commit EMNLP Aug/Sep)\\n- Fallback: AAAI 2027 (abstract July 25, paper Aug 1)\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nLLM-based document-level IE 已逼近 encoder SOTA 的抽取精度，但零方法能生成可验证证据。本文提出 **evidence-grounded DocRE** 作为新任务范式：LLM 同时输出关系三元组和支撑证据句。\\n\\n我们系统性地探索了 online RL (GRPO) 、offline preference optimization (DPO) 和 offline RL (RSFT) 三条技术路线。关键发现：**RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关**——优化强度越大，evidence 质量提升的同时抽取能力退化越严重。QLoRA 放大至 catastrophic（rel_f1<0.02），bf16 GRPO 从 0.4549 退化至 ~0.434（mild, -2pp），DPO 呈 gradual 退化（1ep→3ep rel_f1 从 0.4034→0.3429），RSFT 无 decoupling。\\n\\n**Evidence tax 是 scale-dependent 的（non-monotonic pattern, D079/D082/D083）**：1.7B 模型中 evidence generation 反而带来 +6.2pp 收益（隐式正则化），4B 模型付出 -7.5pp 代价，8B Qwen 模型容量足够 absorb（≈0），8B LLaMA 跨家族确认（-0.7pp）。这推翻了\\\"evidence 总有代价\\\"的简单叙事。\\n\\nCED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination) 是一种 NLI-based 候选筛选函数，通过文档内 hard negative 对比评估 evidence 质量。CED 和 flat-NLI 作为 RSFT scoring functions 统计不可区分（D078 paired t-test p=0.44），但均显著优于 random/no scoring。CED 因可解释性优势作为默认推荐方案。\\n\\n## Story Arc\\n\\n1. **Problem**: LLM DocRE 无证据 → 部署障碍\\n2. **New task**: evidence-grounded DocRE — 同时生成关系和证据\\n3. **Cost of faithfulness**: evidence generation 代价是 **scale-dependent** 的——4B 模型付出 -7.5pp 代价，但 1.7B 反而获益 +6.2pp，8B 趋近于零。这不是固定成本，而是与模型容量的非线性交互（D079/D082/D083）\\n4. **Exploration**: 系统性比较 online RL (GRPO) vs offline preference (DPO) vs offline RL (RSFT)\\n5. **Key finding**: evidence-extraction decoupling — RL optimization pressure 与 decoupling severity 正相关，形成连续谱系（GRPO-QLoRA catastrophic > GRPO-bf16 mild > DPO gradual > RSFT none）\\n6. **QLoRA amplification**: QLoRA 量化将 mild decoupling 放大至 catastrophic forgetting——decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 中 multi-objective competition 的通用梯度（intra-task trade-off，非 inter-task forgetting），QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier\\n7. **What works**: RSFT with NLI-based scoring 是唯一有效路线——提升 evidence 质量而不牺牲抽取\\n8. **Best practical solution**: CED reranker 恢复 97% 的 evidence 代价，同时保留高质量 evidence（evi_f1=0.8188）\\n9. **Diagnostic tool**: EDCR 双层诊断——方法级量化 decoupling（与 Claim 2 呼应），per-relation 级揭示 evi_f1 不捕捉的 relation-type 难度差异（D070 ✅）\\n10. **Scaling & generalization**: evidence tax 呈 non-monotonic scaling pattern（D079/D082/D083），跨模型家族（LLaMA-8B, D082）和跨数据集（Re-DocRED, D080）验证泛化性\\n\\n## Core Claims (reframed after Dream v1, updated D068-D083)\\n\\n### Claim 1 (Main): Evidence-grounded DocRE 是可行的新能力\\n- FREIGE 是首个**基于生成式 LLM** 的 evidence-grounded DocRE 方法（⚠️ 必须始终保持 \\\"generative LLM\\\" 限定词——SAIS 已做 encoder-based evidence retrieval + DocRE）\\n- **关键区分 (Reviewer warning)**：encoder-based evidence *retrieval*（SAIS, DREEAM — 分类哪些句子是证据，evidence supervision 提升 RE）vs generative evidence *production*（FREIGE — LLM 生成证据文本，额外生成负担导致 scale-dependent 代价）。论文 Related Work 必须精准讨论此对比，否则审稿人会问 \\\"为什么 SAIS 说 evidence 有用，你说 evidence 有害\\\"\\n- **Evidence generation tax**: scale-dependent，详见 Claim 5（D079/D082/D083）\\n- RSFT 在 4/4 配置下均超过 SFT baseline（Ign-F1 +3.3~5.5pp）\\n- **CED reranker 是最优实用方案**: rel_f1=0.4864（仅低于 no-evidence 0.29pp），evi_f1=0.8188（plan_005 ✅）\\n- **D076 统一协议最终数字（可写入论文）**:\\n  - SFT baseline: rel_f1=0.4049\\n  - CED RSFT mean: rel_f1=0.4569 (s42=0.4517, s44=0.4621)\\n  - flat-NLI RSFT mean: rel_f1=0.4561 (s42=0.4535, s43_r2=0.4586)\\n  - CED vs flat-NLI Δ=0.0008，统计不可区分（D078 p=0.44）\\n- **Cross-family validation (D082)**: LLaMA-3.1-8B SFT rel_f1=0.5514, evi_f1=0.8151; no-evidence rel_f1=0.5624（evidence tax = -0.7pp，8B 容量足够 absorb）\\n- **Cross-dataset validation (D080)**: Re-DocRED Ign-F1 下降 3.15pp 但 Evi-F1 保持 0.817——evidence extraction 能力跨数据集泛化\\n- 需要 DREEAM-protocol 对齐的 Evi-F1 对比（D041 在处理中）\\n- **RSFT 方法论定位**：诚实承认 RSFT 是 STaR (NeurIPS 2022) 的单轮变体 + evidence quality scoring。创新点在于将 STaR-like self-improvement 应用于 evidence-grounded DocRE + CED/NLI 作为 domain-specific quality filter，而非框架本身\\n- **Evaluation caveat** 1)\\\\n\\\\n## Core Insight\\\\n\\\\nLLM-based document-level IE 已逼近 encoder SOTA 的抽取精度，但零方法能生成可验证证据。本文提出 **evidence-grounded DocRE** 作为新任务范式：LLM 同时输出关系三元组和支撑证据句。\\\\n\\\\n我们系统性地探索了 online RL (GRPO) 、offline preference optimization (DPO) 和 offline RL (RSFT) 三条技术路线。关键发现：**RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关**——优化强度越大，evidence 质量提升的同时抽取能力退化越严重。QLoRA 放大至 catastrophic（rel_f1<0.02），bf16 GRPO 从 0.4549 退化至 ~0.434（mild, -2pp），DPO 呈 gradual 退化（1ep→3ep rel_f1 从 0.4034→0.3429），RSFT 无 decoupling。\\\\n\\\\n**Evidence tax 是 scale-dependent 的（non-monotonic pattern, D079/D082/D083）**：1.7B 模型中 evidence generation 反而带来 +6.2pp 收益（隐式正则化），4B 模型付出 -7.5pp 代价，8B Qwen 模型容量足够 absorb（≈0），8B LLaMA 跨家族确认（-0.7pp）。这推翻了\\\\\\\"evidence 总有代价\\\\\\\"的简单叙事。\\\\n\\\\nCED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination) 是一种 NLI-based 候选筛选函数，通过文档内 hard negative 对比评估 evidence 质量。CED 和 flat-NLI 作为 RSFT scoring functions 统计不可区分（D078 paired t-test p=0.44），但均显著优于 random/no scoring。CED 因可解释性优势作为默认推荐方案。\\\\n\\\\n## Story Arc\\\\n\\\\n1. **Problem**: LLM DocRE 无证据 → 部署障碍\\\\n2. **New task**: evidence-grounded DocRE — 同时生成关系和证据\\\\n3. **Cost of faithfulness**: evidence generation 代价是 **scale-dependent** 的——4B 模型付出 -7.5pp 代价，但 1.7B 反而获益 +6.2pp，8B 趋近于零。这不是固定成本，而是与模型容量的非线性交互（D079/D082/D083）\\\\n4. **Exploration**: 系统性比较 online RL (GRPO) vs offline preference (DPO) vs offline RL (RSFT)\\\\n5. **Key finding**: evidence-extraction decoupling — RL optimization pressure 与 decoupling severity 正相关，形成连续谱系（GRPO-QLoRA catastrophic > GRPO-bf16 mild > DPO gradual > RSFT none）\\\\n6. **QLoRA amplification**: QLoRA 量化将 mild decoupling 放大至 catastrophic forgetting——decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 中 multi-objective competition 的通用梯度（intra-task trade-off，非 inter-task forgetting），QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier\\\\n7. **What works**: RSFT with NLI-based scoring 是唯一有效路线——提升 evidence 质量而不牺牲抽取\\\\n8. **Best practical solution**: CED reranker 恢复 97% 的 evidence 代价，同时保留高质量 evidence（evi_f1=0.8188）\\\\n9. **Diagnostic tool**: EDCR 双层诊断——方法级量化 decoupling（与 Claim 2 呼应），per-relation 级揭示 evi_f1 不捕捉的 relation-type 难度差异（D070 ✅）\\\\n10. **Scaling & generalization**: evidence tax    - Core direction accurately reflects findings ✅
```

```text
   b) The post-hoc retrieval baseline is the critical vulnerability here — if we can achieve comparable results by just doing RE without evidence and then retrieving supporting sentences afterward, that completely undermines the CED-RSFT value proposition, and reviewers will definitely exploit this. This needs to be our top priority.
   
   c) We're wasting GPU capacity with three idle GPUs while the Agent queue is backed up with four pending tasks.
   
   d) The non-monotonic Qwen scaling where the 4B model underperforms is awkward for a scaling study narrative, and the ROADMAP doesn't spell out how we're handling this in the paper.
   
   e) The CED versus flat-NLI equivalence is a contribution problem — if they're indistinguishable, we need to pivot the framing toward CED's interpretability advantages rather than claiming NLI-based scoring as a key novelty.
   
   f) EDCR human validation is still just planned at P1, which leaves us exposed to reviewer criticism on the C4 claim since we're relying only on automated correlation analysis without human validation.
   
   For the EMNLP deadline in about 59 days, the GPU work is manageable at roughly 4 hours of wall time, but the real bottleneck is writing and polishing the paper itself — and we still need to tackle that post-hoc retrieval baseline experiment.
   
   Looking at what's missing from the roadmap: we haven't addressed the corrupted lead2 checkpoint, we're running single-seed evaluations for the new model sizes which is risky for our claims, and there's no strategy documented for explaining the 4B anomaly.
```

   I am the Research Reviewer (独立审查员) for the `faith-evidence-ie` project, Loop #14. My role is to independently verify experiment conclusions, audit GPU compliance, check data consistency, and report findings to Director via `reviewer_post_to_director`. I am read-only — I cannot modify files or run experiments. Key responsibilities this session:

   - Validate newly completed experiment metrics
   - Review ROADMAP update (D088) for research direction reasonableness
   - Identify risks and gaps for the paper (EMNLP 2026 target)

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **CED-RSFT Pipeline**: Contrastive Evidence Discrimination scoring for Rejection Sampling Fine-Tuning on DocRE
   - **D076 Eval Protocol**: batch_size=4, --no-quantize, --sft_adapter, max_new_tokens=1024, seed=42
   - **3-param loading pattern**: --model_path (RSFT ckpt) --base_model (base) --sft_adapter (SFT output)
   - **Scaling Study (C5)**: 1.7B/4B/8B Qwen + LLaMA-3.1-8B × SFT/RSFT matrix
   - **Evidence Tax**: Scale-dependent cost of evidence generation (1.7B benefits +6.2pp, 4B costs -7.0pp, 8B ≈0)
   - **Decoupling Spectrum (C2)**: GRPO-QLoRA catastrophic > GRPO-bf16 mild > DPO gradual > RSFT none
   - **EDCR**: Evidence Distractor Citation Ratio diagnostic metric (ρ=-0.96 with rel_f1)
   - **submit_training_job timeout**: Wrapper uses `timeout N` around commands; was 7200s (too short), fixed to 86400s
   - **EXIT_CODE=124**: Linux timeout signal — process killed by timeout wrapper

   - **ROADMAP.md**: Updated for D088. Contains scaling study progress table, GPU allocation, P0 gap tasks, risk monitoring.
   - **paper_framing.md**: Paper story arc with 5 core claims (C1-C5). Read to verify ROADMAP alignment with paper narrative.

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Systemic timeout bug (EXIT_CODE=124)**: submit_training_job wrapper set `timeout 7200` (2h) but DocRED dev eval (247 batches × 30-50s/batch) needs 8000-10000s. rsft_s44_dev_eval_preds timed out at 240/247 (97%), plan_006_qwen3_1_7b_rsft_eval timed out at 199/247 (81%). **Fix**: Worker changed timeout from 7200 → 86400 (24h). Confirmed in process command line.
   - **Disk full (100% → 91% → 78%)**: Caused lead2 GRPO crash at step 30/50 and 8B RSFT crash at step ~1000/3393. Also corrupted checkpoint-30 (partial safetensors write). **Fix**: Worker cleaned ~33G of old GRPO checkpoints + LLaMA intermediate checkpoints.
   - **lead1_ced_evidence_filter argument error (EXIT_CODE=2)**: Worker used `--predictions_path` but script expects `--predictions`. **Status**: Needs resubmission with correct argument name.
   - **s43 vs s44 model selection**: GPU 0 eval was resubmitted with s43 instead of original s44 (best config). Flagged to Director for confirmation. Director apparently accepted s43.
   - **Missing --gradient_checkpointing in 8B RSFT r2**: New command omitted this flag from original. Training completed successfully anyway (27.5GB/32.6GB, no OOM). Likely the Trainer restored it from saved training_args in checkpoint.
   - **Registry stale state**: 4 experiments marked RUNNING after processes died. Worker eventually updated to DONE/failed with proper failure reasons.

5. Problem Solving:
   - **Identified systemic timeout pattern** by correlating EXIT_CODE=124 with progress percentages and wrapper timeout values across two independent eval jobs
   - **Validated GRPO CED reward claim** by analyzing 6 logged metric entries — trajectory is noisy (0.438-0.599 range) but overall positive trend. Flagged that "+23%" claim needs careful framing in paper.
   - **Built complete scaling study table** by searching registry for all SFT baselines across models:
     - Qwen3-1.7B: SFT 0.4945 → RSFT 0.5219 (+2.7pp)
     - Qwen3-4B: SFT 0.4049 → RSFT 0.4655 (+6.1pp)
     - Qwen3-8B: SFT 0.5262 → RSFT pending eval
     - LLaMA-3.1-8B: SFT 0.5514 → RSFT 0.5674 (+1.6pp)
   - **Identified non-monotonic scaling anomaly**: 1.7B > 4B for both SFT and RSFT in Qwen family. 4B is consistently weakest. Suggested paper strategies (scatter plot instead of curve, or frame as "consistently helps across all scales").
   - **Identified diminishing returns pattern**: RSFT gain inversely correlated with SFT baseline strength (4B +6.1pp from 0.4049, 1.7B +2.7pp from 0.4945, LLaMA +1.6pp from 0.5514)

6. All user messages:

   - **lead1_ced_evidence_filter resubmission**: Fix `--predictions_path` → `--predictions` argument name
   - **lead2 checkpoint-20 eval**: RSFT→GRPO feasibility verification from valid checkpoint

NLI hypothesis 模板：直接用 \"{head} has {relation} with {tail}\" 还是更自然的模板？\n4. 公平性：NLI-based 检索本质上是一个 CED-like 操作——如果 NLI 检索 evi_f1 很高，是否反而说明 CED signal 强但不需要在训练时用？这对我们的 story 有什么影响？\n5. 与 Lead 1（CED 逐句过滤）的关系：Lead 1 是从 RSFT 输出中过滤坏 evidence，post-hoc 是从 no-evidence 输出中检索 evidence——两者方向相反但都用 NLI。\n\n**预期结果**：\n- BM25 应显著弱于 joint generation（表面匹配 vs 隐式推理）\n- NLI 检索可能接近 SFT joint generation 但弱于 RSFT（因为 NLI 不考虑 sentence-pair context）\n- 如果 NLI 检索 evi_f1 ≈ RSFT 的 0.81，则需要重新 frame CED-RSFT 的价值（从\"better evidence\"转向\"training signal quality\"）"

_source line 417 · 2026-05-17T12:29:54.271Z_

[System 触发审查]:
请审查 Post-hoc Retrieval Baseline 实验设计（P0 缺口，审稿人必问）：

**目标**：回答 "为什么需要 joint evidence generation？直接先做 RE 再检索 evidence 不行吗？"

**实验设计**：
1. 用 plan_014 SFT-no-evidence 模型的 predictions（rel_f1=0.507 for 4B，比 with-evidence 的 0.4049 还高）
2. 对每个正确预测的 (head, relation, tail) triple，从源文档中检索 evidence sentences
3. 两种检索方法：
   - **BM25**：query = concat(head_mention, tail_mention)，对文档中每个句子打分，取 top-k
   - **NLI-based**：用 cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base（和 CED 同一个模型），对每个句子计算 entailment score for "sentence entails (head, relation, tail)"，取 top-k
4. 评估：Evi-F1, EDCR, 与 joint generation（SFT with evidence, RSFT）对比

**关键问题请审查**：
1. BM25 query 设计：用 entity mention 还是 entity mention + relation name？relation name 在 DocRED 中是标准化的（如 "headquarters location"），是否给 BM25 不公平的优势？
2. top-k 设置：DocRED 平均每个 triple 有 ~2 个 evidence sentences。用 k=2 还是 k=3？
3. NLI hypothesis 模板：直接用 "{head} has {relation} with {tail}" 还是更自然的模板？
4. 公平性：NLI-based 检索本质上是一个 CED-like 操作——如果 NLI 检索 evi_f1 很高，是否反而说明 CED signal 强但不需要在训练时用？这对我们的 story 有什么影响？
5. 与 Lead 1（CED 逐句过滤）的关系：Lead 1 是从 RSFT 输出中过滤坏 evidence，post-hoc 是从 no-evidence 输出中检索 evidence——两者方向相反但都用 NLI。

**预期结果**：
- BM25 应显著弱于 joint generation（表面匹配 vs 隐式推理）
- NLI 检索可能接近 SFT joint generation 但弱于 RSFT（因为 NLI 不考虑 sentence-pair context）
- 如果 NLI 检索 evi_f1 ≈ RSFT 的 0.81，则需要重新 frame CED-RSFT 的价值（从"better evidence"转向"training signal quality"）

Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — FREIGE\\n\\n> **状态**: POST-MVP + D078-D085 scaling/cross-family/cross-dataset validation complete; RSFT multi-scale pipeline running\\n> **最后更新**: 2026-05-17\\n> **Dream v1**: best=2.5, median=3.0, worst=2.5 → reframe 完成\\n> **Dream v2**: best=3.0, median=2.5, worst=2.5 → best 提升但 median 下降，未达标。关键路径：plan_011 bf16 GRPO eval + structural fixes → re-dream v3\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n\\n- Primary: **EMNLP 2026 Main** (ARR July cycle, ~July 15-25 → commit EMNLP Aug/Sep)\\n- Fallback: AAAI 2027 (abstract July 25, paper Aug 1)\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nLLM-based document-level IE 已逼近 encoder SOTA 的抽取精度，但零方法能生成可验证证据。本文提出 **evidence-grounded DocRE** 作为新任务范式：LLM 同时输出关系三元组和支撑证据句。\\n\\n我们系统性地探索了 online RL (GRPO) 、offline preference optimization (DPO) 和 offline RL (RSFT) 三条技术路线。关键发现：**RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关**——优化强度越大，evidence 质量提升的同时抽取能力退化越严重。QLoRA 放大至 catastrophic（rel_f1<0.02），bf16 GRPO 从 0.4549 退化至 ~0.434（mild, -2pp），DPO 呈 gradual 退化（1ep→3ep rel_f1 从 0.4034→0.3429），RSFT 无 decoupling。\\n\\n**Evidence tax 是 scale-dependent 的（non-monotonic pattern, D079/D082/D083）**：1.7B 模型中 evidence generation 反而带来 +6.2pp 收益（隐式正则化），4B 模型付出 -7.5pp 代价，8B Qwen 模型容量足够 absorb（≈0），8B LLaMA 跨家族确认（-0.7pp）。这推翻了\\\"evidence 总有代价\\\"的简单叙事。\\n\\nCED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination) 是一种 NLI-based 候选筛选函数，通过文档内 hard negative 对比评估 evidence 质量。CED 和 flat-NLI 作为 RSFT scoring functions 统计不可区分（D078 paired t-test p=0.44），但均显著优于 random/no scoring。CED 因可解释性优势作为默认推荐方案。\\n\\n## Story Arc\\n\\n1. **Problem**: LLM DocRE 无证据 → 部署障碍\\n2. **New task**: evidence-grounded DocRE — 同时生成关系和证据\\n3. **Cost of faithfulness**: evidence generation 代价是 **scale-dependent** 的——4B 模型付出 -7.5pp 代价，但 1.7B 反而获益 +6.2pp，8B 趋近于零。这不是固定成本，而是与模型容量的非线性交互（D079/D082/D083）\\n4. **Exploration**: 系统性比较 online RL (GRPO) vs offline preference (DPO) vs offline RL (RSFT)\\n5. **Key finding**: evidence-extraction decoupling — RL optimization pressure 与 decoupling severity 正相关，形成连续谱系（GRPO-QLoRA catastrophic > GRPO-bf16 mild > DPO gradual > RSFT none）\\n6. **QLoRA amplification**: QLoRA 量化将 mild decoupling 放大至 catastrophic forgetting——decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 中 multi-objective competition 的通用梯度（intra-task trade-off，非 inter-task forgetting），QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier\\n7. **What works**: RSFT with NLI-based scoring 是唯一有效路线——提升 evidence 质量而不牺牲抽取\\n8. **Best practical solution**: CED reranker 恢复 97% 的 evidence 代价，同时保留高质量 evidence（evi_f1=0.8188）\\n9. **Diagnostic tool**: EDCR 双层诊断——方法级量化 decoupling（与 Claim 2 呼应），per-relation 级揭示 evi_f1 不捕捉的 relation-type 难度差异（D070 ✅）\\n10. **Scaling & generalization**: evidence tax 呈 non-monotonic scaling pattern（D079/D082/D083），跨模型家族（LLaMA-8B, D082）和跨数据集（Re-DocRED, D080）验证泛化性\\n\\n## Core Claims (reframed after Dream v1, updated D068-D083)\\n\\n### Claim 1 (Main): Evidence-grounded DocRE 是可行的新能力\\n- FREIGE 是首个**基于生成式 LLM** 的 evidence-grounded DocRE 方法（⚠️ 必须始终保持 \\\"generative LLM\\\" 限定词——SAIS 已做 encoder-based evidence retrieval + DocRE）\\n- **关键区分 (Reviewer warning)**：encoder-based evidence *retrieval*（SAIS, DREEAM — 分类哪些句子是证据，evidence supervision 提升 RE）vs generative evidence *production*（FREIGE — LLM 生成证据文本，额外生成负担导致 scale-dependent 代价）。论文 Related Work 必须精准讨论此对比，否则审稿人会问 \\\"为什么 SAIS 说 evidence 有用，你说 evidence 有害\\\"\\n- **Evidence generation tax**: scale-dependent，详见 Claim 5（D079/D082/D083）\\n- RSFT 在 4/4 配置下均超过 SFT baseline（Ign-F1 +3.3~5.5pp）\\n- **CED reranker 是最优实用方案**: rel_f1=0.4864（仅低于 no-evidence 0.29pp），evi_f1=0.8188（plan_005 ✅）\\n- **D076 统一协议最终数字（可写入论文）**:\\n  - SFT baseline: rel_f1=0.4049\\n  - CED RSFT mean: rel_f1=0.4569 (s42=0.4517, s44=0.4621)\\n  - flat-NLI RSFT mean: rel_f1=0.4561 (s42=0.4535, s43_r2=0.4586)\\n  - CED vs flat-NLI Δ=0.0008，统计不可区分（D078 p=0.44）\\n- **Cross-family validation (D082)**: LLaMA-3.1-8B SFT rel_f1=0.5514, evi_f1=0.8151; no-evidence rel_f1=0.5624（evidence tax = -0.7pp，8B 容量足够 absorb）\\n- **Cross-dataset validation (D080)**: Re-DocRED Ign-F1 下降 3.15pp 但 Evi-F1 保持 0.817——evidence extraction 能力跨数据集泛化\\n- 需要 DREEAM-protocol 对齐的 Evi-F1 对比（D041 在处理中）\\n- **RSFT 方法论定位**：诚实承认 RSFT 是 STaR (NeurIPS 2022) 的单轮变体 + evidence quality scoring。创新点在于将 STaR-like self-improvement 应用于 evidence-grounded DocRE + CED/NLI 作为 domain-specific quality filter，而非框架本身\\n- **Evaluation caveat** (Limitations): GenRES 指出 exact match F1 低估 generative RE，FREIGE 所有 eval 用 exact match，应承认此局限（对所有方法同等适用，相对比较有效）\\n\\n### Claim 2 (Core finding): RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关（D068/D069 修正）\\n- **Decoupling spectrum**: 优化强度越大，decoupling 越严重\\n  - GRPO QLoRA: catastrophic（rel_f1=0.0138, evi_f1=0.850, EDCR=0.99）— QLoRA 放大 decoupling\\n  - GRPO bf16: mild（rel_f1 从 0.4549→~0.434, -2pp; step_30=0.434, step_50=0.4276, step_70=0.4341）— 退化可控但持续（D065/D066）\\n  - DPO bf16 1ep→3ep: gradual（Ign-F1 38.88→TBD, rel_f1 0.4034→0.3429）— 随训练渐进退化（D067/D068）\\n  - RSFT: none — 无 decoupling，唯一有效路线\\n- **QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier，不是 root cause**: bf16 GRPO 仅 mild decoupling（-2pp），QLoRA 放大至 catastrophic（-39pp）（D054/D065/D066）\\n- **Decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 的通用梯度**: 跨方法（GRPO/DPO）、跨量化策略（QLoRA/bf16）一致出现，严重程度与优化压力正相关\\n- 三档 Dream 审稿均认为此发现\\\\\\\"genuinely novel and striking\\\\\\\"\\n- **已完成**: GRPO G=8 bf16 确认 mild decoupling（plan_011 ✅）; DPO 1ep/3ep 确认 gradual decoupling（plan_012 ✅）\\n\\n### Claim 3 (Ablation): NLI-based scoring 提升 RSFT 质量——CED 与 flat-NLI 统计不可区分（D078）\\n- CED 和 flat-NLI 都显著优于 random/no scoring\\n- **D078 paired t-test 最终结论**: CED vs flat-NLI p=0.44，统计不可区分\\n  - CED RSFT mean rel_f1=0.4569 vs flat-NLI mean rel_f1=0.4561（Δ=0.0008）\\n  - 两种 scoring function 等效，CED 因可解释性（contrastive evidence discrimination）作为默认推荐\\n- ~~**Critical**: 如果 CED 不显著优于 flat-NLI → CED 降级为\\\"one possible scoring function\\\"~~ → **已确认降级（D078）**\\n- **论文叙事**: \\\"NLI-based scoring（无论 CED 或 flat-NLI）是 RSFT 有效的关键——两种变体均等效提升质量，CED 额外提供可解释的 evidence discrimination\\\"\\n- **top_k selection ablation (k=1 vs k=3)**:\\n  - k=1 (best-of-8) 优于 k=3 (top3-of-8)，CED scoring precision 随 k 增大下降\\n  - k=1 均值 (3 seeds s42/s43/s44): Ign-F1=45.00, rel_f1=0.4596, evi_f1=81.43, EDCR=0.6434\\n  - k=3 (1 seed, reeval_rsft_topk3): Ign-F1=43.64, rel_f1=0.4454, evi_f1=80.88, EDCR=0.6635\\n  - Δ (k=3 vs k=1 mean): Ign-F1 **-1.36pp**, rel_f1 **-1.42pp**, evi_f1 **-0.55pp**, EDCR +0.02\\n  - 训练数据质量下降: k=1 mean_f1=0.6157, mean_ced=0.6683 → k=3 mean_f1=0.5761, mean_ced=0.6205（来源: mvp2_rsft_ced_scoring / mvp2_rsft_ced_scoring_topk3_r2）\\n  - 训练集规模: k=1 选出 2718 样本 (12.4%), k=3 选出 8037 样本 (37%), 数据量 ×2.96 但质量下降抵消数量优势\\n  - **叙事**: 低排名 response 引入噪声——rank 2-3 样本的 F1 和 CED 分数均显著低于 rank 1，扩大训练集规模无法补偿数据质量的稀释。这表明 RSFT 的关键不在数据量而在 scoring precision\\n  - **注**: k=3 仅 1 seed，差异方向一致但尚不具备统计显著性；作为 ablation table 中的辅助行呈现，不作为独立 claim\\n\\n### Claim 4 (Diagnostic): EDCR 双层诊断价值（D070/D072）\\n- **Layer 1 — 方法级**: EDCR 量化 evidence-extraction decoupling，与 Claim 2 spectrum 呼应\\n  - 方法级 EDCR 与 rel_f1 Spearman ρ=-0.965（plan_003 ✅）——本质是 decoupling 的度量\\n  - EDCR 识别病理模型（GRPO QLoRA: EDCR≈1.0）\\n- **Layer 2 — per-relation-type**: EDCR 提供 evi_f1 不捕捉的 relation-type 诊断信息 ✅\\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, evi_f1) = **-0.064** (p=0.58) → **完全独立于 evi_f1**\\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, rel_f1) = -0.793 (p<0.001) → 与 rel_f1 强相关但非完美\\n  - per-relation ρ(evi_f1, rel_f1) = -0.013 (p=0.91) → evidence 质量与抽取能力在 relation type 间完全解耦\\n  - 典型案例：date_of_birth (EDCR=0.118, 高 faithful) vs head_of_government (EDCR=0.956, 高 decoupled)\\n  - 93 relation types 分析（≥5 样本），76 个有非零数据\\n- **论文叙事**: EDCR 在方法级是 decoupling 度量工具（横向对比方法），在 relation 级是难度诊断工具（纵向定位薄弱 relation）——两层叙事互补\\n- **仍需**: 100 例人工标注验证（RC6）\\n\\n### Claim 5 (NEW — Scaling): Evidence Tax 呈 Non-Monotonic Scaling Pattern（D079/D082/D083）\\n- **核心发现**: evidence generation 的代价不是固定的——它与模型容量呈非线性关系\\n  - **1.7B (Qwen3-1.7B)**: +6.2pp BENEFIT — evidence generation 起隐式正则化作用，防止小模型过拟合（D083 公平子集分析确认）\\n  - **4B (Qwen3-4B)**: -7.5pp cost — 标准 evidence generation 负担，模型容量不足以同时优化两个目标\\n  - **8B Qwen (Qwen3-8B)**: ≈0 — 模型容量足够 absorb evidence generation 的额外负担\\n  - **8B LLaMA (LLaMA-3.1-8B)**: -0.7pp — 跨家族确认，8B 规模的 evidence tax 趋近于零\\n- **Non-monotonic 解释**: 小模型从 evidence 的正则化效应获益 > 生成负担；中等模型容量不足以同时做好两件事；大模型容量充裕，两个目标互不干扰\\n- **论文叙事**: 推翻 \\\"evidence 总有代价\\\" 的简单叙事，揭示 evidence generation 与模型容量的非线性交互——这为选择何种规模模型部署 evidence-grounded IE 提供实际指导\\n- **Cross-family robustness**: LLaMA-8B 与 Qwen-8B 一致确认 8B 规模 evidence tax ≈ 0\\n\\n## MVP Evidence Summary (D071: 主表报 Ign-F1，附录报 F1; D076 统一协议 、offline preference optimization (DPO) 和 offline RL (RSFT) 三条技术路线。关键发现：**RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关**——优化强度越大，evidence 质量提升的同时抽取能力退化越严重。QLoRA 放大至 (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination) 是一种 NLI-based 候选筛选函数，通过文档内 hard negative 对比评估 evidence 质量。CED 和 flat-NLI 作为 RSFT scoring functions 统计不可区分（D078 paired t-test p=0.44），但均显著优于 random/no scoring。CED **scale-dependent** 的——4B 模型付出 -7.5pp 代价，但 1.7B 反而获益 +6.2pp，8B 趋近于零。这不是固定成本，而是与模型容量的非线性交互（D079/D082/D083）\\\\n4. **Exploration**: 系统性比较 online RL (GRPO) vs offline preference (DPO) vs offline RL **QLoRA amplification**: QLoRA 量化将 mild decoupling 放大至 catastrophic forgetting——decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 中 multi-objective competition 的通用梯度（intra-task trade-off，非 inter-task forgetting），QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier\\\\n7. **What works**: RSFT with NLI-based scoring 是唯一有效路线——提升 evidence 质量而不牺牲抽取\\\\n8. **Best practical solution**: CED reranker 恢复 97% 的 evidence 代价，同时保留高质量 3.15pp 但 Evi-F1 保持 0.817——evidence extraction 能力跨数据集泛化\\\\n- 需要 DREEAM-protocol 对齐的 Evi-F1 对比（D041 在处理中）\\\\n- **RSFT 方法论定位**：诚实承认 RSFT 是 STaR (NeurIPS 2022) 的单轮变体 + evidence quality scoring。创新点在于将 STaR-like self-improvement 应用于 evidence-grounded DocRE + CED/NLI 作为 domain-specific quality filter，而非框架本身\\\\n- **Evaluation caveat** (Limitations): GenRES 指出 exact match F1 低估 generative RE，FREIGE 所有 eval 用 exact match，应承认此局限（对所有方法同等适用，相对比较有效）\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 2 (Core finding): RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关（D068/D069 修正）\\\\n- **Decoupling spectrum**: 优化强度越大，decoupling 越严重\\\\n  - GRPO QLoRA: catastrophic（rel_f1=0.0138, evi_f1=0.850, EDCR=0.99）— QLoRA 放大 decoupling\\\\n  - GRPO bf16: mild（rel_f1 从 0.4549→~0.434, -2pp; step_30=0.434, step_50=0.4276, amplifier，不是 root cause**: bf16 GRPO 仅 mild decoupling（-2pp），QLoRA 放大至 catastrophic（-39pp）（D054/D065/D066）\\\\n- **Decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 的通用梯度**: 跨方法（GRPO/DPO）、跨量化策略（QLoRA/bf16）一致出现，严重程度与优化压力正相关\\\\n- 三档 Dream 审稿均认为此发现\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"genuinely novel and striking\\\\\\\\\\\\\\\"\\\\n- **已完成**: GRPO G=8 bf16 确认 mild decoupling（plan_011 ✅）; DPO 1ep/3ep 确认 gradual decoupling（plan_012 ✅）\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 3 (Ablation): NLI-based scoring 提升 RSFT 质量——CED 与 flat-NLI 统计不可区分（D078）\\\\n- CED 和 flat-NLI 都显著优于 random/no scoring\\\\n- **D078 paired - Δ (k=3 vs k=1 mean): Ign-F1 **-1.36pp**, rel_f1 **-1.42pp**, evi_f1 **-0.55pp**, EDCR +0.02\\\\n  - 训练数据质量下降: k=1 mean_f1=0.6157, mean_ced=0.6683 → k=3 mean_f1=0.5761, mean_ced=0.6205（来源: 分数均显著低于 rank 1，扩大训练集规模无法补偿数据质量的稀释。这表明 RSFT 的关键不在数据量而在 scoring precision\\\\n  - **注**: k=3 仅 1 seed，差异方向一致但尚不具备统计显著性；作为 ablation table 中的辅助行呈现，不作为独立 claim\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 4 (Diagnostic): EDCR 双层诊断价值（D070/D072）\\\\n- **Layer 1 — 方法级**: EDCR 量化 evidence-extraction decoupling，与 Claim 2 spectrum 呼应\\\\n  - 方法级 EDCR 与 rel_f1 Spearman ρ=-0.965（plan_003 ✅）——本质是 decoupling 的度量\\\\n  - EDCR 识别病理模型（GRPO QLoRA: EDCR≈1.0）\\\\n- **Layer 2 — per-relation-type**: EDCR 提供 evi_f1 不捕捉的 relation-type 诊断信息 ✅\\\\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, evi_f1) = **-0.064** (p=0.58) → **完全独立于 evi_f1**\\\\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, rel_f1) = -0.793 (p<0.001) → 与 rel_f1 强相关但非完美\\\\n  - per-relation ρ(evi_f1, rel_f1) = -0.013 (p=0.91) → evidence 质量与抽取能力在 relation type 间完全解耦\\\\n  - 典型案例：date_of_birth (EDCR=0.118, 高 faithful) vs head_of_government (EDCR=0.956, 高 decoupled)\\\\n  - 93 relation types 分析（≥5 样本），76 个有非零数据\\\\n- **论文叙事**: EDCR 在方法级是 decoupling 度量工具（横向对比方法），在 relation 级是难度诊断工具（纵向定位薄弱 relation）——两层叙事互补\\\\n- **仍需**: 100 例人工标注验证（RC6）\\\\n\\\\n### Claim 5 (NEW — Scaling): Evidence Tax 呈 Non-Monotonic Scaling Pattern（D079/D082/D083）\\\\n- **核心发现**: evidence generation 的代价不是固定的——它与模型容量呈非线性关系\\\\n  - **1.7B (Qwen3-1.7B)**: +6.2pp BENEFIT — evidence generation 起隐式正则化作用，防止小模型过拟合（D083 公平子集分析确认）\\\\n  - **4B (Qwen3-4B)**: -7.5pp cost — 标准 evidence generation FREIGE\\n\\n> **状态**: POST-MVP + D078-D085 scaling/cross-family/cross-dataset validation complete; RSFT multi-scale pipeline running\\n> **最后更新**: 2026-05-17\\n> **Dream v1**: best=2.5, median=3.0, worst=2.5 → reframe 完成\\n> **Dream v2**: best=3.0, median=2.5, worst=2.5 → best 提升但 median 下降，未达标。关键路径：plan_011 bf16 GRPO eval + structural fixes → re-dream v3\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n\\n- Primary: **EMNLP 2026 Main** (ARR July cycle, ~July 15-25 → commit EMNLP Aug/Sep)\\n- Fallback: AAAI 2027 (abstract July 25, paper Aug 1)\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nLLM-based document-level IE 已逼近 encoder SOTA 的抽取精度，但零方法能生成可验证证据。本文提出 **evidence-grounded DocRE** 作为新任务范式：LLM 同时输出关系三元组和支撑证据句。\\n\\n我们系统性地探索了 online RL (GRPO) 、offline preference optimization (DPO) 和 offline RL (RSFT) 三条技术路线。关键发现：**RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关**——优化强度越大，evidence 质量提升的同时抽取能力退化越严重。QLoRA 放大至 catastrophic（rel_f1<0.02），bf16 GRPO 从 0.4549 退化至 ~0.434（mild, -2pp），DPO 呈 gradual 退化（1ep→3ep rel_f1 从 0.4034→0.3429），RSFT 无 decoupling。\\n\\n**Evidence tax 是 scale-dependent 的（non-monotonic pattern, D079/D082/D083）**：1.7B 模型中 evidence generation 反而带来 +6.2pp 收益（隐式正则化），4B 模型付出 -7.5pp 代价，8B Qwen 模型容量足够 absorb（≈0），8B LLaMA 跨家族确认（-0.7pp）。这推翻了\\\"evidence 总有代价\\\"的简单叙事。\\n\\nCED (Contrastive Evidence Discrimination) 是一种 NLI-based 候选筛选函数，通过文档内 hard negative 对比评估 evidence 质量。CED 和 flat-NLI 作为 RSFT scoring functions 统计不可区分（D078 paired t-test p=0.44），但均显著优于 random/no scoring。CED 因可解释性优势作为默认推荐方案。\\n\\n## Story Arc\\n\\n1. **Problem**: LLM DocRE 无证据 → 部署障碍\\n2. **New task**: evidence-grounded DocRE — 同时生成关系和证据\\n3. **Cost of faithfulness**: evidence generation 代价是 **scale-dependent** 的——4B 模型付出 -7.5pp 代价，但 1.7B 反而获益 +6.2pp，8B 趋近于零。这不是固定成本，而是与模型容量的非线性交互（D079/D082/D083）\\n4. **Exploration**: 系统性比较 online RL (GRPO) vs offline preference (DPO) vs offline RL (RSFT)\\n5. **Key finding**: evidence-extraction decoupling — RL optimization pressure 与 decoupling severity 正相关，形成连续谱系（GRPO-QLoRA catastrophic > GRPO-bf16 mild > DPO gradual > RSFT none）\\n6. **QLoRA amplification**: QLoRA 量化将 mild decoupling 放大至 catastrophic forgetting——decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 中 multi-objective competition 的通用梯度（intra-task trade-off，非 inter-task forgetting），QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier\\n7. **What works**: RSFT with NLI-based scoring 是唯一有效路线——提升 evidence 质量而不牺牲抽取\\n8. **Best practical solution**: CED reranker 恢复 97% 的 evidence 代价，同时保留高质量 evidence（evi_f1=0.8188）\\n9. **Diagnostic tool**: EDCR 双层诊断——方法级量化 decoupling（与 Claim 2 呼应），per-relation 级揭示 evi_f1 不捕捉的 relation-type 难度差异（D070 ✅）\\n10. **Scaling & generalization**: evidence tax 呈 non-monotonic scaling pattern（D079/D082/D083），跨模型家族（LLaMA-8B, D082）和跨数据集（Re-DocRED, D080）验证泛化性\\n\\n## Core Claims (reframed after Dream v1, updated D068-D083)\\n\\n### Claim 1 (Main): Evidence-grounded DocRE 是可行的新能力\\n- FREIGE 是首个**基于生成式 LLM** 的 evidence-grounded DocRE 方法（⚠️ 必须始终保持 \\\"generative LLM\\\" 限定词——SAIS 已做 encoder-based evidence retrieval + DocRE）\\n- **关键区分 (Reviewer warning)**：encoder-based evidence *retrieval*（SAIS, DREEAM — 分类哪些句子是证据，evidence supervision 提升 RE）vs generative evidence *production*（FREIGE — LLM 生成证据文本，额外生成负担导致 scale-dependent 代价）。论文 Related Work 必须精准讨论此对比，否则审稿人会问 \\\"为什么 SAIS 说 evidence 有用，你说 evidence 有害\\\"\\n- **Evidence generation tax**: scale-dependent，详见 Claim 5（D079/D082/D083）\\n- RSFT 在 4/4 配置下均超过 SFT baseline（Ign-F1 +3.3~5.5pp）\\n- **CED reranker 是最优实用方案**: rel_f1=0.4864（仅低于 no-evidence 0.29pp），evi_f1=0.8188（plan_005 ✅）\\n- flat-NLI Δ=0.0008，统计不可区分（D078 p=0.44）\\n- **Cross-family validation (D082)**: LLaMA-3.1-8B SFT rel_f1=0.5514, evi_f1=0.8151; no-evidence rel_f1=0.5624（evidence tax = -0.7pp，8B 容量足够 absorb）\\n- **Cross-dataset validation (D080)**: Re-DocRED Ign-F1 下降 3.15pp 但 Evi-F1 保持 0.817——evidence extraction 能力跨数据集泛化\\n- 需要 DREEAM-protocol 对齐的 Evi-F1 对比（D041 在处理中）\\n- **RSFT 方法论定位**：诚实承认 RSFT 是 STaR (NeurIPS 2022) 的单轮变体 + evidence quality scoring。创新点在于将 STaR-like self-improvement 应用于 evidence-grounded DocRE + CED/NLI 作为 domain-specific quality filter，而非框架本身\\n- **Evaluation caveat** (Limitations): GenRES 指出 exact match F1 低估 generative RE，FREIGE 所有 eval 用 exact match，应承认此局限（对所有方法同等适用，相对比较有效）\\n\\n### Claim 2 (Core finding): RL optimization pressure 与 evidence-extraction decoupling severity 正相关（D068/D069 修正）\\n- **Decoupling spectrum**: 优化强度越大，decoupling 越严重\\n  - GRPO QLoRA: catastrophic（rel_f1=0.0138, evi_f1=0.850, EDCR=0.99）— QLoRA 放大 decoupling\\n  - GRPO bf16: mild（rel_f1 从 0.4549→~0.434, -2pp; step_30=0.434, step_50=0.4276, step_70=0.4341）— 退化可控但持续（D065/D066）\\n  - DPO bf16 1ep→3ep: gradual（Ign-F1 38.88→TBD, rel_f1 0.4034→0.3429）— 随训练渐进退化（D067/D068）\\n  - RSFT: none — 无 decoupling，唯一有效路线\\n- **QLoRA 是 catastrophic amplifier，不是 root cause**: bf16 GRPO 仅 mild decoupling（-2pp），QLoRA 放大至 catastrophic（-39pp）（D054/D065/D066）\\n- **Decoupling 是 RL-for-IE 的通用梯度**: 跨方法（GRPO/DPO）、跨量化策略（QLoRA/bf16）一致出现，严重程度与优化压力正相关\\n- 三档 Dream 审稿均认为此发现\\\\\\\"genuinely novel and striking\\\\\\\"\\n- **已完成**: GRPO G=8 bf16 确认 mild decoupling（plan_011 ✅）; DPO 1ep/3ep 确认 gradual decoupling（plan_012 ✅）\\n\\n### Claim 3 (Ablation): NLI-based scoring 提升 RSFT 质量——CED 与 flat-NLI 统计不可区分（D078）\\n- CED 和 flat-NLI 都显著优于 random/no scoring\\n- **D078 paired t-test 最终结论**: CED vs flat-NLI p=0.44，统计不可区分\\n  - CED RSFT mean rel_f1=0.4569 vs flat-NLI mean rel_f1=0.4561（Δ=0.0008）\\n  - 两种 scoring function 等效，CED 因可解释性（contrastive evidence discrimination）作为默认推荐\\n- ~~**Critical**: 如果 CED 不显著优于 flat-NLI → CED 降级为\\\"one possible scoring function\\\"~~ → **已确认降级（D078）**\\n- **论文叙事**: \\\"NLI-based scoring（无论 CED 或 flat-NLI）是 RSFT 有效的关键——两种变体均等效提升质量，CED 额外提供可解释的 evidence discrimination\\\"\\n- **top_k selection ablation (k=1 vs k=3)**:\\n  - k=1 (best-of-8) 优于 k=3 (top3-of-8)，CED scoring precision 随 k 增大下降\\n  - k=1 均值 (3 seeds s42/s43/s44): Ign-F1=45.00, rel_f1=0.4596, evi_f1=81.43, EDCR=0.6434\\n  - k=3 (1 seed, reeval_rsft_topk3): Ign-F1=43.64, rel_f1=0.4454, evi_f1=80.88, EDCR=0.6635\\n  - Δ (k=3 vs k=1 mean): Ign-F1 **-1.36pp**, rel_f1 **-1.42pp**, evi_f1 **-0.55pp**, EDCR +0.02\\n  - 训练数据质量下降: k=1 mean_f1=0.6157, mean_ced=0.6683 → k=3 mean_f1=0.5761, mean_ced=0.6205（来源: mvp2_rsft_ced_scoring / mvp2_rsft_ced_scoring_topk3_r2）\\n  - 训练集规模: k=1 选出 2718 样本 (12.4%), k=3 选出 8037 样本 (37%), 数据量 ×2.96 但质量下降抵消数量优势\\n  - **叙事**: 低排名 response 引入噪声——rank 2-3 样本的 F1 和 CED 分数均显著低于 rank 1，扩大训练集规模无法补偿数据质量的稀释。这表明 RSFT 的关键不在数据量而在 scoring precision\\n  - **注**: k=3 仅 1 seed，差异方向一致但尚不具备统计显著性；作为 ablation table 中的辅助行呈现，不作为独立 claim\\n\\n### Claim 4 (Diagnostic): EDCR 双层诊断价值（D070/D072）\\n- **Layer 1 — 方法级**: EDCR 量化 evidence-extraction decoupling，与 Claim 2 spectrum 呼应\\n  - 方法级 EDCR 与 rel_f1 Spearman ρ=-0.965（plan_003 ✅）——本质是 decoupling 的度量\\n  - EDCR 识别病理模型（GRPO QLoRA: EDCR≈1.0）\\n- **Layer 2 — per-relation-type**: EDCR 提供 evi_f1 不捕捉的 relation-type 诊断信息 ✅\\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, evi_f1) = **-0.064** (p=0.58) → **完全独立于 evi_f1**\\n  - per-relation ρ(EDCR, rel_f1) = -0.793 (p<0.001) → 与 rel_f1 强相关但非完美\\n  - per-relation ρ(evi_f1, rel_f1) = -0.013 (p=0.91) → evidence 质量与抽取能力在 relation type 间完全解耦\\n  - 典型案例：date_of_birth (EDCR=0.118, 高 faithful) vs head_of_government (EDCR=0.956, 高 decoupled)\\n  - 93 relation types 分析（≥5 样本），76 个有非零数据\\n- **论文叙事**: EDCR 在方法级是 decoupling 度量工具（横向对比方法），在 relation 级是难度诊断工具（纵向定位薄弱 relation）——两层叙事互补\\n- **仍需**: 100 例人工标注验证（RC6）\\n\\n### Claim 5 (NEW — Scaling): Evidence Tax 呈 Non-Monotonic Scaling Pattern（D079/D082/D083）\\n- **核心发现**: evidence generation 的代价不是固定的——它与模型容量呈非线性关系\\n  - **1.7B (Qwen3-1.7B)**: +6.2pp BENEFIT — evidence generation 起隐式正则化作用，防止小模型过拟合（D083 公平子集分析确认）\\n  - **4B (Qwen3-4B)**: -7.5pp cost — 标准 evidence generation 负担，模型容量不足以同时优化两个目标\\n  - **8B Qwen (Qwen3-8B)**: ≈0 — 模型容量足够 absorb evidence generation 的额外负担\\n  - **8B LLaMA (LLaMA-3.1-8B)**: -0.7pp — 跨家族确认，8B 规模的 evidence tax 趋近于零\\n- **Non-monotonic 解释**: 小模型从 evidence 的正则化效应获益 > 生成负担；中等模型容量不足以同时做好两件事；大模型容量充裕，两个目标互不干扰\\n- **论文叙事**: 推翻 \\\"evidence 总有代价\\\" 的简单叙事，揭示 evidence generation 与模型容量的非线性交互——这为选择何种规模模型部署 evidence-grounded IE 提供实际指导\\n- **Cross-family robustness**: LLaMA-8B 与 Qwen-8B 一致确认 8B 规模 evidence tax ≈ 0\\n\\n## MVP Evidence Summary (D071: 主表报 Ign-F1，附录报 F1; D076 统一协议 <details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
OK, now I have the key data:

For 4B no-evidence: rel_f1 = 0.4876 (F1), ign_f1 = 0.4721
For 4B SFT with evidence: rel_f1 = 0.4049 (from paper_framing)

Wait, the design doc says "rel_f1=0.507 for 4B" but the registry shows f1=0.4876. Let me check which metric they mean. The paper_framing uses "rel_f1" as the standard metric. 

Looking at the plan_014 D076 eval more carefully, I see the registry entry has ign_f1 at 0.4721 and f1 at 0.4876, which align with the paper_framing's SFT no-evidence row showing Ign-F1 = 47.36 and F1 = 48.93 — these percentages match the 4B numbers when converted.

There seem to be different eval runs with slight variations, but I have the key data points I need: 4B SFT with evidence at rel_f1 = 0.4049, 4B SFT no-evidence at f1 = 0.4876, and 1.7B SFT with evidence at rel_f1 = 0.4945, which gives me enough to review the post-hoc retrieval baseline design. So for 1.7B, the +6.2pp benefit means evidence actually helps, giving me a no-evidence baseline of around 0.4325. But for 4B, the -7.5pp cost flips the calculation—with evidence at 0.4049, the no-evidence version would be closer to 0.4799, which aligns with the d076 eval showing 0.4876.

There's a discrepancy though: the design request mentions rel_f1=0.507 for 4B no-evidence, but the unified protocol shows different numbers (0.4876 f1 / 0.4721 ign_f1), so I should flag that inconsistency.

Now looking at the actual design choices, I'm considering how to structure the BM25 queries—whether to use just entity mentions or combine them with standardized relation names like "headquarters location" from DocRED.

The key insight is that this is post-hoc retrieval applied after the model already predicts the relation, so using the relation name isn't really giving BM25 an unfair advantage in that sense. However, relation names in DocRED are quite informative and might directly match evidence sentences in ways that wouldn't happen with more implicit relations in practice. I think the right approach is to test both configurations and report the results for both, so we can see the actual impact.

For the top-k setting, since DocRED has about 2 evidence sentences per triple on average, k=2 makes the most sense as the baseline. I could also test k=3 to see if there's a meaningful difference, and use oracle k (matching the ground truth count for each individual triple) as an upper bound to understand the ceiling. For the NLI hypothesis template, the simple "{head} has {relation} with {tail}" format doesn't work well for DocRED's relations like "headquarters location" or "date of birth" — they need relation-specific templates or a more natural phrasing to actually sound grammatical.

Creating 96 separate templates would be too costly, so I could either have the LLM generate the templates dynamically, or use a universal template like "The relationship between {head} and {tail} is {relation}." The real issue is that the NLI model's performance depends heavily on template quality, so if the template is poor, the whole comparison becomes unfair. I should test at least two different templates and report whichever performs better, or even try using the raw triplet representation directly without a fixed template.

Now I'm thinking through the fairness concern — whether using NLI for retrieval is actually comparable to the CED approach in RSFT.

The key distinction is that NLI retrieval finds evidence for triples that are already correct, whereas RSFT trains the model to generate evidence as part of its output. Even if NLI retrieval achieves similar evidence F1 scores, it has a fundamental limitation: it only works when the triple itself is predicted correctly. For incorrect triples, you either get no evidence or misleading evidence, which is a different problem than what RSFT solves.

Joint generation, on the other hand, produces evidence for every prediction the model makes. This serves multiple purposes: it gives users visibility into why the model made each prediction (even wrong ones), the quality of the evidence becomes a signal about how confident the model should be, and you avoid needing a separate retrieval step. If NLI retrieval ends up matching RSFT's evidence F1, it might suggest the model is learning to do something equivalent to NLI-based evidence identification internally, but the real value of RSFT is that it accomplishes this evidence reasoning alongside relation extraction in a single forward pass, rather than as a post-hoc retrieval step.

Looking at how this connects to the CED filtering approach: post-hoc retrieval adds evidence sentences to model outputs, while CED filtering removes bad evidence from RSFT outputs—opposite directions but both leveraging NLI. The framing becomes clearer now: post-hoc is the simplest baseline, RSFT trains the model to generate evidence jointly, and applying CED filtering on top of RSFT gives you the best of both worlds by cleaning up the model's evidence while keeping the joint training benefits.

But I'm noticing a potential evaluation issue that could skew the comparison. The post-hoc approach retrieves evidence only for correct predictions, which naturally inflates its evidence F1 score. Meanwhile, RSFT evidence metrics are computed across all predicted triples—both correct and incorrect ones—which would naturally lower the evidence F1 since you're including evidence for wrong predictions. This creates an unfair comparison unless the evaluation protocol is consistent. I need to verify whether both approaches are being evaluated on the same set of triples, or if the standard DocRE evaluation already accounts for this by only computing evidence F1 on correctly predicted triples.

There's also a complication: the no-evidence model produces a different set of correct predictions than the with-evidence model, so post-hoc retrieval would be working with a different (and potentially larger) set of triples to evaluate. This further muddies the comparison.

I'm also noticing a metric discrepancy—the design reports rel_f1=0.507 for the 4B no-evidence baseline, but the unified eval shows f1=0.4876 / ign_f1=0.4721. I need to clarify which number is the authoritative one before finalizing my review.
```

```text
Actually, comparing the rel_f1 values directly: 0.5219 for RSFT minus 0.4945 for SFT gives 0.0842, which is nowhere near the +6.2pp benefit the paper claims. That's a significant discrepancy. Let me check if the paper_framing is actually based on ign_f1 instead. I should focus on the key issues for the review: there's a metric mismatch where the design document shows rel_f1=0.507 for the 4B no-evidence baseline, but D076 reports f1=0.4876 and ign_f1=0.4721, which don't align.

For the main critique points, I need to push back on several methodological choices. The BM25 query setup should test both entity-only and entity+relation to avoid accusations of unfair comparison. For top-k, using oracle k as an upper bound plus k=2 as the practical setting gives a complete picture. The NLI template choice is critical—different templates can dramatically skew results, so multiple templates must be tested. The evaluation protocol itself is problematic: computing evi_f1 on different triple sets between no-evidence and RSFT predictions isn't apples-to-apples, so either the same triple set should be used or joint F1 should be reported. Finally, if NLI retrieval performance matches RSFT's evi_f1, that reframes the whole story—it's not about training signal quality but rather about joint generation being a one-pass convenience with evidence serving as a confidence signal.

The three approaches actually form a nice spectrum: post-hoc retrieval as addition, RSFT as joint generation, and Lead1 as subtraction from RSFT. That's a compelling way to structure the narrative.
```

```json
{
  "text": "[severity: warning] Post-hoc Retrieval Baseline 实验设计审查\n\n## 总体评价\n\n设计方向正确——这确实是审稿人必问的问题，且 P0 优先级合理。但设计中有几个问题需要在执行前修正，否则实验结果要么不可信、要么反过来伤害自己的 story。\n\n## 具体问题\n\n### 1. [severity: warning] 数据引用不一致\n\n设计说\"rel_f1=0.507 for 4B\"。registry D076 统一协议（plan_014_no_evidence_eval_d076）实际数字：\n- f1=0.4876, ign_f1=0.4721\n\n0.507 来源不明。如果用的是旧 eval 结果而非 D076 统一协议，与其他 scaling study 数字不可比。**必须统一使用 D076 数字**。\n\n### 2. [severity: critical] 评估协议对齐问题——最大风险\n\n设计说\"对每个**正确预测**的 triple 检索 evidence\"。但这引入了一个根本性的公平性问题：\n\n- **no-evidence 4B 模型**：f1=0.4876 → 正确 triple 集合 A\n- **SFT with-evidence 4B 模型**：rel_f1=0.4049 → 正确 triple 集合 B（更小）\n- **RSFT 4B 模型**：rel_f1=0.4601 → 正确 triple 集合 C\n\n三个模型的正确 triple 集合不同。post-hoc 在集合 A 上检索 evidence，RSFT 在集合 C 上生成 evidence——evi_f1 计算基数不同，直接比较无意义。\n\n**修复方案**：\n- 用 **joint F1**（同时要求 triple 和 evidence 都对）作为统一指标——这自然处理了不同 triple 集合的问题\n- 或者在 triple 交集（A∩C）上比较 evi_f1\n\n### 3. [severity: warning] BM25 query 设计\n\n同意设计中对 relation name 泄漏的担忧。DocRED relation 名如\"headquarters location\"过于语义化，BM25 直接匹配会获得不公平优势。\n\n**建议**：同时测 entity-only 和 entity+relation 两版。报告两者，用 entity-only 作主对比，entity+relation 作 upper bound。这避免两个方向的批评：\"baseline 太弱\"和\"baseline 有信息泄漏\"。\n\n### 4. [severity: warning] NLI hypothesis 模板质量决定实验成败\n\n\"{head} has {relation} with {tail}\" 对大量 DocRED relation 不自然（如 date_of_birth → \"Einstein has date_of_birth with 1879\"？）。模板差 → NLI 打分差 → 结论变成\"NLI 模板写得差所以 retrieval 不行\"而非\"retrieval 本身不行\"。\n\n**建议**：\n- 至少测 2 个模板：(a) 简单拼接 \"{head}, {relation}, {tail}\" (b) 更自然的 \"The {relation} of {head} is {tail}\"\n- 报告较好的那个——确保 NLI 在最佳模板下的性能是 post-hoc 方法的真实上界\n\n### 5. [severity: info] top-k 设置\n\nDocRED 每个 triple 平均 ~2 evidence sentences，但方差较大（1-5+）。建议：\n- 固定 k=2 和 k=3 作为实际设置\n- Oracle k（= ground truth evidence 数量）作为上界\n- Oracle k 结果非常重要：如果 NLI + oracle-k 的 evi_f1 仍明显低于 RSFT，说明 post-hoc 方法有天花板\n\n### 6. [severity: critical] NLI retrieval 高性能的 story 影响——必须提前想清楚\n\n设计预期\"如果 NLI 检索 evi_f1 ≈ RSFT 的 0.81，需要重新 frame\"。这个分析不充分。\n\n**如果 NLI post-hoc evi_f1 ≈ RSFT evi_f1**，正确的 framing 不是\"training signal quality\"，而是：\n\n(a) **Joint generation 的不可替代价值**：post-hoc 只能给正确 triple 加 evidence。Joint generation 为**所有** predicted triples 生成 evidence——包括错误预测。Evidence 质量本身是预测可信度的信号（高 EDCR = 低可信度），这是 post-hoc 做不到的。\n\n(b) **单 pass 效率**：post-hoc 需要两阶段（predict → retrieve），joint generation 一阶段完成。这在实际部署中有价值。\n\n(c) **RSFT 的真正贡献**：不是\"生成更好的 evidence\"（post-hoc NLI 也能做到），而是\"训练模型同时做 RE + evidence generation 而不 decouple\"——控制 evidence tax。\n\n**建议**：无论 NLI 结果如何，把 post-hoc retrieval 定位为\"evidence retrieval upper bound\"，然后论证 joint generation 有 post-hoc 不具备的附加价值（confidence signal, one-pass, 全 triple 覆盖）。\n\n### 7. [severity: info] 与 Lead 1 的关系\n\n三种 evidence 策略构成清晰的谱系：\n- Post-hoc（加法）：predict → retrieve evidence\n- RSFT（联合）：jointly generate RE + evidence\n- Lead 1（减法）：RSFT output → CED filter 移除坏 evidence\n\n论文可以把这个谱系作为一个分析框架呈现，而不仅是对比。\n\n## 审查结论\n\n设计整体方向对，但 **评估协议对齐**（#2）和 **story 预案**（#6）是 critical 问题。执行前必须：\n1. 明确 joint F1 作为统一对比指标\n2. 准备好\"NLI 高性能\"和\"NLI 低性能\"两种结果的 paper framing 预案\n3. 统一使用 D076 数字",
```
